    need=int(DT[gi]-init_down[gi])\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        for t in range(min(T,need)):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            add_row([(u[gi,t],1.0)], 0.0, 0.0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h# min up/down\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hfor gi in range(G):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    ut=int(UT[gi]); dt=int(DT[gi])\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    for t in range(T):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        k0=max(0,t-ut+1)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        add_row([(startup[gi,k],1.0) for k in range(k0,t+1)] + [(u[gi,t],-1.0)], -INF, 0.0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        k0=max(0,t-dt+1)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        add_row([(shutdown[gi,k],1.0) for k in range(k0,t+1)] + [(u[gi,t],1.0)], -INF, 1.0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h# ramping + reserve deliverability\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hfor gi in range(G):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    for t in range(T):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if t==0:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            prev_p=float(init_p[gi]); prev_u=float(init_u[gi])\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            # p+r - SU*startup <= prev_p + RU*prev_u\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            add_row([(p[gi,t],1.0),(rsv[gi,t],1.0),(startup[gi,t],-SU[gi])], -INF, prev_p + RU[gi]*prev_u)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            # -p - RD*u - SD*shutdown <= -prev_p\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            add_row([(p[gi,t],-1.0),(u[gi,t],-RD[gi]),(shutdown[gi,t],-SD[gi])], -INF, -prev_p)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        else:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            prev_p_idx=p[gi,t-1]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            prev_u_idx=u[gi,t-1]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            add_row([(p[gi,t],1.0),(rsv[gi,t],1.0),(prev_p_idx,-1.0),(prev_u_idx,-RU[gi]),(startup[gi,t],-SU[gi])], -INF, 0.0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            add_row([(prev_p_idx,1.0),(p[gi,t],-1.0),(u[gi,t],-RD[gi]),(shutdown[gi,t],-SD[gi])], -INF, 0.0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h# off recursion\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hfor gi in range(G):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    for t in range(1,T):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        prev_u_idx=u[gi,t-1]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        add_row([(off[gi,t],1.0),(off[gi,t-1],-1.0),(prev_u_idx,-M_OFF)], -INF, 1.0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        add_row([(off[gi,t],-1.0),(off[gi,t-1],1.0),(prev_u_idx,-M_OFF)], -INF, -1.0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        add_row([(off[gi,t],1.0),(prev_u_idx,M_OFF)], -INF, M_OFF)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h# tier selection\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hfor gi in range(G):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    lags=lag_list[gi]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    tg=len(lags)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    lnext=lags[1:]+[10**6]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    for t in range(T):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        add_row([(z[gi][t,s],1.0) for s in range(tg)] + [(startup[gi,t],-1.0)], 0.0, 0.0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        for s in range(tg):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            zz=z[gi][t,s]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            add_row([(zz,1.0),(startup[gi,t],-1.0)], -INF, 0.0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            add_row([(off[gi,t],-1.0),(zz,float(lags[s]))], -INF, 0.0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            upper=float(lnext[s]-1)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            add_row([(off[gi,t],1.0),(zz,M_OFF)], -INF, upper+M_OFF)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h# demand + reserve\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hfor t in range(T):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    add_row([(p[gi,t],1.0) for gi in range(G)] + [(pr[ri,t],1.0) for ri in range(R)], float(demand[t]), float(demand[t]))\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    add_row([(rsv[gi,t],-1.0) for gi in range(G)], -INF, -float(reserve_req[t])) \u001b[A\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[C\u001b[K)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hA=coo_matrix((vals,(rows,cols)), shape=(row,n)).tocsr()\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hres=milp(\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    c=c,\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    integrality=integ,\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    bounds=Bounds(lb,ub),\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    constraints=LinearConstraint(A,np.array(clb),np.array(cub)),\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    options={'time_limit': 1800.0, 'mip_rel_gap': 5e-3, 'disp': False},\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hif res.x is None:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    raise RuntimeError(f'No incumbent: status={res.status} msg={res.message}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hx=res.x\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hmsg=str(res.message).lower()\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hif res.status==0:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    solver_status='optimal'\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004helif 'time' in msg and 'limit' in msg:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    solver_status='time_limit_feasible'\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004helse:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    solver_status='feasible'\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hu_sol=x[u].reshape(G,T); st_sol=x[startup].reshape(G,T); sh_sol=x[shutdown].reshape(G,T)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hu_bin=np.rint(u_sol).astype(int); st_bin=np.rint(st_sol).astype(int); sh_bin=np.rint(sh_sol).astype(int)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hif np.max(np.abs(u_sol-u_bin))>1e-5 or np.max(np.abs(st_sol-st_bin))>1e-5 or np.max(np.abs(sh_sol-sh_bin))>1e-5:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    raise RuntimeError('Non-integer solution returned')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hp_sol=x[p].reshape(G,T)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hr_sol=x[rsv].reshape(G,T)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hpr_sol=x[pr].reshape(R,T)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h# recompute costs and validate\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hdef prod_cost_from_curve(gi, mw):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    pts=therm[gi]['piecewise_production']\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    xs=[float(pt['mw']) for pt in pts]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    ys=[float(pt['cost']) for pt in pts]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    if mw <= xs[0] + 1e-8:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        return ys[0]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    if mw >= xs[-1] - 1e-8:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        return ys[-1]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    for (x0,y0),(x1,y1) in zip(list(zip(xs,ys))[:-1], list(zip(xs,ys))[1:]):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if x0-1e-8 <= mw <= x1+1e-8:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            a=(mw-x0)/(x1-x0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            return y0 + a*(y1-y0)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    raise ValueError('mw outside curve')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hdef startup_cost_for(gi, t):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    if t==0:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        d=int(init_down[gi]) if init_u[gi]==0 else 0\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    else:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        d=0\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        tau=t-1\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        while tau>=0 and u_bin[gi,tau]==0:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            d += 1\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            tau -= 1\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if tau<0 and init_u[gi]==0:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            d += int(init_down[gi])\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    chosen=0\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    for i,L in enumerate(lag_list[gi]):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if d>=L:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            chosen=i\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        else:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            break\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    return cost_list[gi][chosen]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hmax_dem_v=0.0\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hmax_res_short=0.0\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hfor gi in range(G):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    if must_run[gi]==1 and not np.all(u_bin[gi,:]==1):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        raise RuntimeError(f'must-run violated {g_names[gi]}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    if init_u[gi]==1 and init_up[gi] < UT[gi]:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        need=int(UT[gi]-init_up[gi])\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if not np.all(u_bin[gi,:min(T,need)]==1):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            raise RuntimeError(f'init min-up violated {g_names[gi]}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    if init_u[gi]==0 and init_down[gi] < DT[gi]:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        need=int(DT[gi]-init_down[gi])\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if not np.all(u_bin[gi,:min(T,need)]==0):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            raise RuntimeError(f'init min-down violated {g_names[gi]}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    prev_u_val=int(init_u[gi])\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    prev_p_val=float(init_p[gi])\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    for t in range(T):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if u_bin[gi,t]-prev_u_val != st_bin[gi,t]-sh_bin[gi,t]:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            raise RuntimeError(f'transition fail {g_names[gi]} t={t}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if u_bin[gi,t]==0:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            if abs(p_sol[gi,t])>1e-4 or abs(r_sol[gi,t])>1e-4:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h                raise RuntimeError(f'offline nonzero {g_names[gi]} t={t}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        else:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            if p_sol[gi,t] < pmin[gi]-1e-4 or p_sol[gi,t] > pmax[gi]+1e-4:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h                raise RuntimeError(f'limits {g_names[gi]} t={t}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            if p_sol[gi,t]+r_sol[gi,t] > pmax[gi]+1e-4:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h                raise RuntimeError(f'headroom {g_names[gi]} t={t}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        # ramp checks\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        lhs_up=(p_sol[gi,t]+r_sol[gi,t]) - prev_p_val\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        rhs_up=RU[gi]*prev_u_val + SU[gi]*st_bin[gi,t]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if lhs_up > rhs_up + 1e-4:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            raise RuntimeError(f'ramp up {g_names[gi]} t={t}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        lhs_dn=prev_p_val - p_sol[gi,t]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        rhs_dn=RD[gi]*u_bin[gi,t] + SD[gi]*sh_bin[gi,t]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if lhs_dn > rhs_dn + 1e-4:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            raise RuntimeError(f'ramp down {g_names[gi]} t={t}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        prev_u_val=int(u_bin[gi,t])\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        prev_p_val=float(p_sol[gi,t])\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    for t in range(T):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if st_bin[gi,t]==1:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            for tau in range(t, min(T, t+int(UT[gi]))):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h                if u_bin[gi,tau]!=1:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h                    raise RuntimeError(f'min up {g_names[gi]} start={t} tau={tau}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if sh_bin[gi,t]==1:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            for tau in range(t, min(T, t+int(DT[gi]))):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h                if u_bin[gi,tau]!=0:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h                    raise RuntimeError(f'min down {g_names[gi]} shut={t} tau={tau}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hif not (np.all(pr_sol + 1e-6 >= ren_min) and np.all(pr_sol - 1e-6 <= ren_max)):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    raise RuntimeError('renewable bounds violated')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hhourly_summary=[]\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hfor t in range(T):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    th=float(np.sum(p_sol[:,t])); rn=float(np.sum(pr_sol[:,t])); dem=float(demand[t]); resv=float(np.sum(r_sol[:,t]))\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    max_dem_v=max(max_dem_v, abs(th+rn-dem))\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    max_res_short=max(max_res_short, max(0.0, float(reserve_req[t]-resv)))\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    hourly_summary.append({\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        'hour': int(t+1),\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        'demand_MW': dem,\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        'thermal_generation_MW': th,\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        'renewable_generation_MW': rn,\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        'reserve_requirement_MW': float(reserve_req[t]),\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        'scheduled_spinning_reserve_MW': resv,\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    })\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hif max_dem_v > 1e-3 or max_res_short > 1e-3:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    raise RuntimeError('system balance/reserve violated')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hobj=0.0\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hfor gi in range(G):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    for t in range(T):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if u_bin[gi,t]==1:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            obj += prod_cost_from_curve(gi, float(p_sol[gi,t]))\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if st_bin[gi,t]==1:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            obj += startup_cost_for(gi,t)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hif res.fun is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    if abs(obj-float(res.fun))>1e-2:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        raise RuntimeError(f'cost mismatch recomputed {obj} solver {res.fun}')\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h# gap\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hreported_gap=None\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hfor key in ['mip_gap','relative_gap']:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    if hasattr(res,key):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        v=getattr(res,key)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        if v is not None and np.isfinite(v):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h            reported_gap=float(v)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hif reported_gap is None:\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    dual=getattr(res,'mip_dual_bound',None)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    if dual is not None and np.isfinite(dual) and res.fun is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        prim=float(res.fun)\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h        reported_gap=max(0.0, abs(prim-float(dual))/max(1.0,abs(prim)))\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004hthermal_report=[{\n\u001b[?2004l\u001b[?2004h    'name':
